# Re:Zero — master longitudinal ledger

## 0. Authority, history, and use

This is the canonical cumulative state ledger for the Re:Zero Japanese main-light-novel analysis. It is mutable current authority through the latest verified volume transaction. Frozen volume readings remain the authority for what was supportable at each historical prospective horizon and for their detailed source-facing arguments.

This ledger was created after V02 during the project-initiation architecture repair. The V01/V02 state below is a controlled backfill from the already frozen canonical readings; the ledger did not govern those readings prospectively and neither freeze was modified. Exact source evidence was consulted only where necessary to validate the source identities already recorded by those artifacts.

For every new volume, update this file only after the source-facing reading has identified the material transitions. Preserve revision ancestry and route to the relevant VNN section or exact EPUB locus. Do not duplicate an independently promoted ledger here beyond a routing/current-summary row.

### V05-to-V06 transition

V06 §11 adjudicates all 48 V05 claims, and §13 all 32 entering questions, without changing their earlier evidence. Puck's giant identity is confirmed; unseen old E1 freeze remains inferred. Rem's mission and specific envoy transfer become known; medical price and admission purpose remain separate. ### V06-to-V07 transition

V07 §11 adjudicates all sixty V06 claims, §13 all forty inherited questions, and §2 all ten entering forecasts without editing the earlier freeze. The same active state continues with no new Subaru death. The alliance and Whale hunt succeed; erased victims do not return. Chosen trust, pressured risks, conditional self-killing, differentiated care and qualified Julius repair replace any universal recovery or betrayal model. Wilhelm–Theresia history adds a bounded case, not a complete person. ### V07-to-V08 transition

V08 §12 adjudicates all seventy-two V07 claims, §14 all forty-five questions and §2 all ten forecasts. The operation is enacted, suffers real losses and resource destruction, receives Emilia's defense and ends in Subaru's eleventh death after possession. Physical body death remains sound while the multiple-Sloths hypothesis is rejected for one parasitic mental entity. Care includes enemy rescue, requested lethal use and a bounded self-recovery exception; neither universal resurrection nor universal mental immunity follows. Cooperation deepens without a finished peer conversation, Emilia reunion or unpressured civilian consent. Eighty-two claims and fifty V09 questions are frozen; four existing specialist owners suffice. No successor state, capital checkpoint, mature person or non-spine admission is invented.

### V08-to-V09 transition

V09 §12 individually adjudicates all82 V08 claims, §14 all50 inherited questions and §2 all10 entering forecasts. The observed council return and later actual death twelve/bedside return replace an unlocated successor with RZ-E-012/RZ-E-013, without mutualizing the old terminal attempt. New Julius trust, received Emilia apology/love and current civilian work are real; covert constraints, borrowed capacities and animal costs remain. Petelgeuse's final entity extinction is explicit, but a separate convoy attack leaves living Rem unresponsive/lost from tested others' memory and Crusch without her own past. | Mimi / Garfiel | offers closeness despite unreturned affection, then next morning blushes/pulls from a headpat and chooses nearer-but-not-too-near distance | affection and touch boundaries change by moment; support need not require reciprocal romance | wrist hurt/apology remain; no blanket touch/risk consent, sex or completed couple; V17-B17–19 |
| Mimi | reports unknown parents/likely abandonment, a finder called ローシ and chosen family with Anastasia/commander | own account differentiates belonging from a mascot role | motive is her inference, finder identity/exact age/full childhood unknown; V17-B19 |

No independent ordinary-life ledger is promoted. New crisis evidence does not erase the broad earlier noncrisis record; nor does one sweet, nickname, robe or gesture furnish a complete mundane biography.

V20 B19, p-007/p-008 B0001–0060. |

| Theresia / Wilhelm — repeated meetings | Yellow-flower meetings and the slow acquisition of his name make attention and curiosity ordinary before the decisive conflict. | An actual developing attachment is more than an invariant rescued-wife role; no earlier mutual love confessed by proxy. V20 B19, p-008 B0060–0260. |
| Theresia / Wilhelm — sword purpose | Her recurring questions meet his changing account of why he fights; saving him is both chosen care and a blow to his pride. | The relationship contains conflict and received refusal before the later promise; no deliberately thrown final duel. V20 B19–20, p-008 B0060–0392. |

| Theresia — retired ordinary life | The couple’s calm life and garden promise coexist with anxiety because the blessing remains; Wilhelm’s embrace is narrated comfort. | Retirement changes practice and desire rather than automatically cancelling a power; no universal consent inferred from marriage. V20 B20, p-008 B0393–0409. |

V20 B20, p-008 B0410–0456. |

| Luanna / royal brother Ford / kidnapped girl / brothers / unnamed gardener or visitor | Separate reported/historical roles and exact named/unnamed references | A familiar spelling/title does not identify every missing person, diagnose theft or establish a current reply |

These judgments preserve meaningful bounded work rather than waiting for complete biographies or declaring every important person mature. No additional monograph, final relationship/mechanics/ethics/institutional specialist or full-series artifact is earned by prominence, victimhood, file symmetry or count.

#### Current architecture and mandatory work order

The five sole owners remain sufficient: eventV2.8/E021/20Subaru deaths/21segments; knowledgeV2.8/K761; mechanicsV1.17/M482; institutionV1.21/I403; relationshipV1.15/R171/RT406. Master continues to own423current claims, people, ordinary/voice increments, prospective bodies and qualitative readiness. Existing source-facing bundles, direct locators, actual narrow checks and byte-preserving edit plans handled the new distinctions. Adjacent draft routes and attribution were corrected within those homes; those findings do not by themselves earn a sixth ledger, new field or independent evidence/locator index.

Complete substantive candidates must be measured before declaring any storage move. If an active maintained file would reach1MiB, select genuinely historical spans, preserve complete exact preimages and original-heading routers, and prove the native inverse under the unchanged limit. This is storage maintenance, not a new analytical owner or permission to reconstruct a document approximately.

**Required sequence:** V20/PR360, ARC_05_CHECKPOINT/PR363, THERESIA20/PR364 and REINHARD20/PR367 full gates CLOSED → locally accepted FELT_V01_V20_MODEL_AND_FIDELITY_REVIEW/own full gate PENDING → V21 against unchanged sealed prospective body → continuous admitted sequence throughV43 and mandatory terminal architecture/state/role-gap/acquisition review.

Felt is implemented at exact local same-analyst nonblind documentary acceptance; its own complete publication/integration gate is still required. All seventeen earlier model/review/default/local scopes remain exact. The existing nineteen-dimension/confidence/rationale contract stays unchanged; no independent/held-out/runtime/global grade follows. CI handles merges. No ordinary-unit/model pause, future narrative admission or terminal goal closure follows.

Historical V20 storage decision (not current candidate measurements): The complete substantive master measured1427922bytes before storage selection; the two whole historical claim generations require469088fenced bytes against archive01's404588available bytes. The declared lossless move uses archive01/02 at the master link base, with original-heading routers and full native inverse. The resulting named draft master is959770bytes; it is a candidate measurement, not the committed final-blob certificate. Both incoming seals remain literal in master. The complete changelog candidate is1007835bytes, so no changelog rollover is required.
